How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

(part 30/96)

# Collectable Items
Categories: Items, Special items
Collectables are items that are rare or impossible to obtain, or mainly sought after for reasons other than utility. Although some have practical uses, this does not necessarily exclude them from being a collectable if they would otherwise be considered one. They can be tokens of achievement, luck, presence in a period of time, or simply as a cosmetic item. Most collectables are [[Special Items]] or [[Glitched Items]].

These Collectables are rewarded repeatedly throughout every SkyBlock year or event.

## One Time Rewards
These Collectables are rewarded only one time, they may be repurchased from [[Rusty]] or from their source once given.
|Icon|Name|Source|
|-|-|-|
|[[Polished Pebble]]|[[Polished Pebble]]|Reward from [[Gustave]] for finishing [[Woods Race]] in under 2 Minutes.|
|[[Enderman Mask]]|[[Enderman Mask]]|Reward from [[Guber]] for finishing [[End Race]] in under 2 Minutes.|
|[[Dark Pebble]]|[[Dark Pebble]]|Reward from [[Gunther]] for finishing [[Rift Race]] in under 60 Seconds.|
|[[Doubloon of the Family]]|[[Doubloon of the Family]]|An item obtained from [[Inverted Sirius]], can be sold for 100,000 [[Coins]].|
|[[Wallet]]|[[Wallet]]|An item obtained from killing [[Detective Amog]], can be sold for 500,000 [[Coins]].|
|[[Bag of Gold]]|[[Bag of Gold]]|An item obtained from completing the quests from [[Rollim]] and [[Kaus]], can be sold for 250,000 [[Coins]].|
|[[Dwarven Tankard]]|[[Dwarven Tankard]]|Reward for typing "burp" in the public chat after talking with one of the NPCs in the [[Dwarven Tavern]].|
|[[The Cake]]|[[The Cake]]|Reward for talking with [[Royal Resident]] after a long dialogue.|
|[[Kuudra Follower Armor]]|[[Kuudra Follower Armor]]|Each Armor Piece is rewarded for completing certain tasks under certain conditions, used to obtain the [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]].|
|[[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]]|[[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]]|An item given alongside [[Kuudra Follower Relic]] after completing certain tasks under certain conditions.|

# Crimson Hunter Set
Aliases: Crimson Equipment, Crimson Hunter Equipment
Categories: Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Equipment, Crimson Isle
[Crimson Hunter Set]
Type: Item
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Vanquisher Set]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BLAZE_BELT; GHAST_CLOAK; MAGMA_NECKLACE; GLOWSTONE_GAUNTLET
Tooltip: Defense: +7; Strength: +7; Ability: Blazing Palisade Gain a 10% chance to reflect Fireballs.; Right-click to equip!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 22.; RARE BELT
Crimson Hunter Set is a group of four different [[Equipment]] pieces: [[Blaze Belt]], [[Ghast Cloak]], [[Magma Necklace]], and [[Glowstone Gauntlet]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Blaze Belt]]|Blaze Rod IV (4) ([[Blaze Rod]])|12x [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] 25x [[Blaze Ashes]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] x3 ×4 + [[Blaze Ashes]] x25 → [[Blaze Belt]]|
|[[Ghast Cloak]]|Ghast Tear IV (4) ([[Ghast Tear]])|10x [[Tentacle Meat]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] x32 ×6 + [[Tentacle Meat]] x10 → [[Ghast Cloak]]|
|[[Magma Necklace]]|Magma Cream IV (4) ([[Magma Cream]])|12x [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] 20x [[Magma Chunk]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] x3 ×4 + [[Magma Chunk]] x20 → [[Magma Necklace]]|
|[[Glowstone Gauntlet]]|Glowstone Dust IV (4) ([[Glowstone Dust]])|9x [[Enchanted Leather]] 12x [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Leather]] x3 ×3 + [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]] x2 ×6 → [[Glowstone Gauntlet]]|
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Belt|Cloak|
|-|-|
|[[Blaze Belt]]; [[Defense]]: +7 [[Strength]]: +7 Ability: Blazing Palisade; Gain a 10% chance to reflect Fireballs.|[[Ghast Cloak]]; [[Health]]: +7 [[Defense]]: +7 Ability: Blazing Restoration; Restores 5 [[Health]] every second while worn.|
|Necklace|Gloves|
|[[Magma Necklace]]; [[Health]]: +15 Ability: Cube Fanatic; Gain 10% more Magma Cream when killing Magma Cubes.|[[Glowstone Gauntlet]]; [[Intelligence]]: +10 [[Mining Fortune]]: +5|
[Total]
[Vanquisher Set Health: +22 Defense: +14 Strength: +7 Intelligence: +10 Mining Fortune: +5]
### Crafting
The Crimson Hunter Set can be upgraded to the [[Vanquisher Set]]. Upgrading keeps [[Enchantments]], [[Reforging|Reforges]], [[Recombobulator 3000]] and [[Attributes]].
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Vanquished Blaze Belt]]|Blaze Rod VII (7) ([[Blaze Rod]])|1x [[Blaze Belt]] 2x [[Enchanted Blaze Rod]] 3x [[Nether Star]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Blaze Rod]] ×2 + [[Blaze Belt]] + [[Nether Star]] ×3 → [[Vanquished Blaze Belt]]|
|[[Vanquished Ghast Cloak]]|Ghast Tear VII (7) ([[Ghast Tear]])|1x [[Ghast Cloak]] 3x [[Nether Star]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Silver Fang]]|Recipe: [[Nether Star]] ×3 + [[Silver Fang]] x64 ×4 + [[Ghast Cloak]] → [[Vanquished Ghast Cloak]]|
|[[Vanquished Magma Necklace]]|Magma Cream VII (7) ([[Magma Cream]])|1x [[Magma Necklace]] 3x [[Nether Star]] 256x [[Enchanted Magma Cream]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] x64 ×4 + [[Magma Necklace]] + [[Nether Star]] x3 → [[Vanquished Magma Necklace]]|
|[[Vanquished Glowstone Gauntlet]]|Glowstone Dust VII (7) ([[Glowstone Dust]])|1x [[Glowstone Gauntlet]] 3x [[Nether Star]] 4x [[Enchanted Glowstone]]|Recipe: [[Nether Star]] ×3 + [[Enchanted Glowstone]] ×4 + [[Glowstone Gauntlet]] → [[Vanquished Glowstone Gauntlet]]|

The Critical Potion is a BUFF [[Potions|Potion]] that temporarily increases the player's [[Crit Chance]] and [[Crit Damage]].
## Stats
Potion:
Critical I Potion: Critical Ⅰ → Ⅳ
3:00: Increases [[Crit Chance]] by 10% → 25% and [[Crit Damage]] by 10% → 40%.

[→ → →]

### Brewing

# David's Cloak
Aliases: David Cloak, David cloak, Davids Cloak
Categories: Items, Equipment, Necklaces, Legendary items, Mythic items
[David's Cloak]
Type: Cloak ([[Cloaks]])
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])–MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 50; [[Magic Find]]: 2
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DAVIDS_CLOAK
Tooltip: Health: +50; Magic Find: +2; The more you Hunt, the stronger this cloak gets!; Talk to David to view your progress!; Right-click to equip!; This item can be reforged!; * Soulbound * COMMON CLOAK
David's Cloak is a [[Rarity|COMMON]]–[[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Cloaks|Cloak]]. It upgrades upon reaching [[Hunting]] milestones.
## Obtaining
David's Cloak can be claimed from [[David Hunterborough]] inside the [[Fusion House]] after completing his training. Once the player has claimed their cloak, they can talk to [[David Hunterborough]] to view their milestones.
David's Cloak can be re-purchased from [[Rusty]] in case the original is lost.
## Usage
Cloak:
David's Cloak [[Health]]: +50 [[Magic Find]]: +2
The stats of David's Cloak improve based on [[Hunting]] milestones. These are for:
- Unlocking unique [[Attributes]]
- Syphoning [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] and gaining [[Hunting Fortune]]
- Hunting Combat creatures
- Hunting Water creatures
- Hunting Forest creatures
- Obtaining [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] from [[Huntraps]]
- Obtaining [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] from [[Salts]]
The [[Attributes|Cloak Improvement]] Attribute, obtained from syphoning [[Tortoise Shard|Tortoise Shards]], grants +1-10 [[True Defense]] on the David's Cloak.

## Trivia
- The Chesto Berry is a reference to Pokémon, which has a berry of the same name.
- This Ancient Fruit is a reference to one of the crops from the game Stardew Valley.
- Lettuce and Onion can be used to obtain a [[Great Spook Artifact]] during the [[Tyashoi Alchemist|Alchemy Experiments]].
- During the [[Tyashoi Alchemist|Alchemy Experiments]], Appalled Pumpkin will transform into a [[Wet Pumpkin]] after being submerged in water, which can be used to obtain a [[Great Spook Armor|Great Spook Helmet]].

## Obtaining
### Purchase
A Dungeon Potion can be purchased from [[Ophelia]] for [[Coins]].
Cost:
- Tier Ⅰ: 5,000 [[Coins]]
- Tier Ⅱ: 10,000 [[Coins]]
- Tier Ⅲ: 15,000 [[Coins]]
- Tier Ⅳ: 20,000 [[Coins]]
- Tier Ⅴ: 25,000 [[Coins]]
- Tier Ⅵ: 30,000 [[Coins]]
- Tier Ⅶ: 35,000 [[Coins]]
### Mob Drops
Speedy Blood room Mobs drop dungeon potions with the same tier as the floor they are in. These potions have 1 higher speed potion effect level than dungeon potions from Ophelia.
#### Modifying
Certain features can extend the length of the potion.

|Ingredient|Effect|
|-|-|
|[[Potion Affinity Talisman]]|+10% Duration|
|[[Potion Affinity Ring]]|+25% Duration|
|[[Potion Affinity Artifact]]|+50% Duration|
|[[Parrot Pet]]|+40% Duration|
|[[Alchemy]]|+𝑥 Duration|

# Dyes
Aliases: Armor Dye, Armor Dyes, Armour Dye, Armour Dyes, Bingo Dye, Custom Dyes, Cyclamen, Dye, Dyes (disambiguation)
Categories: Items, Pages missing IDs, Cosmetics, Dye
[Dyes]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]]), LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]), MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Changes the color of an armor piece to #71A6D2!; LEGENDARY DYE
Custom Dyes are items used to change the color of vanilla [[Armor]] pieces. They are obtained from performing various actions in the game, and most of them are very rare.
## Usage
Dyes can be combined with any vanilla armor piece (i.e., not helmets that are a skull item) in an [[Anvil]] for 100 [[Bits]] to change their appearance.
The armor piece will have a new icon next to its name, with the color of the dye displayed at the bottom. This helps distinguish it from [[Exotics|Exotic]] armor.
Dyes can also be donated to [[Vincent]] to obtain a [[Bucket of Dye]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] with the color of the donated dye.

### Animated Dyes
Animated Dyes are special dyes that transition smoothly between set colors when applied to an armor piece. They are purchased from [[Fire Sale|Fire Sales]] and [[Taylor]]'s shop, with each dye costing 375 [[SkyBlock Gems]]. Animated Dyes cannot be used to create a [[Bucket of Dye]].
Due to the nature of [[Fire Sale|Fire Sales]], these dyes become unobtainable once they are sold out.

## Trivia
- Helmets that had a skin applied cannot be dyed, even if they were originally vanilla helmets and not skull items.
- [[Necron Dye]] used to be the only custom dye that has [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity.
- The 1.21+ Skyblocker/SkyOcean mod allows players to apply custom armor dyes client-side using the brush in the user's inventory. This will not be seen by other players.
- Armor colored by Dyes is marked with the ✿ symbol next to the item's name and displays the Dye's name above the item's rarity.
  - This distinguishes dyed armor from regular or [[Exotics|Exotic]] armor.

# Enchanted Book
Aliases: Enchantment Book
Categories: Items, Article stubs, Pages under review, Enchanted, Mob Drops, Scam-Prone Items, Enchants
[Enchanted Book]
Type: Consumable
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]]) / UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) / RARE ([[Rarity]]) / EPIC ([[Rarity]]) / LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) / MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: [[Enchanting]]
Uses: [[Enchantments]]
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: Yes ([[Anvil]] only)
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Librarian]] [[Pearl Dealer]] [[Melancholic Viking]] [[Tomioka]] [[Old Shaman Nyko]]
Buy: 20–25,000,000 [[Coins]] (depending on [[Enchantments]] and [[NPC]])
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 1x [[Book]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTED_BOOK
Tooltip: Use this on an item in an Anvil to apply it!; COMMON
Enchanted Books are [[Rarity|COMMON]], [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]], [[Rarity|RARE]], [[Rarity|EPIC]], [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], or [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] items, with its rarity being dependent on the [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] and its corresponding level. They are obtained through [[Experiments]], certain mob drops, or through crafting.
## Overview
Certain Enchanted Books can be combined with each other in an [[Anvil]] to increase its level, as such:
- [[Enchanted Book]] 2 Enchanted Book ([[Protection|Protection III]]) can be combined into a single [[Enchanted Book]] Enchanted Book ([[Protection|Protection IV]]).
- Combining a [[Helix Chopper]] with [[Karma|Karma IV]] with an [[Enchanted Book]] Enchanted Book ([[Karma|Karma IV]]) yields a [[Helix Chopper]] with [[Karma|Karma V]].
- Enchanted Books that contain Enchantments that cannot be obtained from an [[Enchantment Table]] (e.g. [[Luck|Luck VI]], [[Protection|Protection VI]], etc.) cannot be combined to increase its level.
- Certain Enchantment tiers can only be obtained through [[Endcaps]], which must be combined in an [[Anvil]] with the desired item.
## Rarity Scaling
|Level Range|Rarity|
|-|-|
|1-4|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|
|5|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|
|6|RARE ([[Rarity]])|
|7|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|
|8|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|
|9-10|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|
There are some exceptions to this, for example a Replenish I book is Rare.
## Bazaar
### Enchantments
The Books for Enchantments that cannot be obtained through an [[Enchantment Table]] can be bought and sold on the [[Bazaar]].
- [[Enchanted Book/Bazaar (Normal)|Tab 1]]
### Ultimate Enchantments
Ultimate Enchantment Books can be bought and sold on the [[Bazaar]].
- [[Enchanted Book/Bazaar (Ultimate)|Tab 1]]

## Trivia
- When an enchanted book has two enchantments for different tools, an example being Giant Killer and Efficiency, it will use the enchantment based on the tool it is used on. For example, applying it on a pickaxe will not give the pickaxe Giant Killer.
- It used to be possible to obtain enchanted books without any enchantments through mostly unknown means, one of them being old [[Telekinesis (Enchantment)|Telekinesis]] books having their enchantments removed. Some players still have these glitched books.

Enchanted Book Bundles are [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]-[[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] distributable packages which contain one or more of the listed [[Enchanted Book|Enchanted Books]].
## List

|[[Enchanted Book Bundle]]|Rainbow III ([[Rainbow]])|1|[[Raffle of the Century]]|

|[[Enchanted Book Bundle]]|Small Brain III ([[Small Brain]])|1|[[Raffle of the Century]]|

|[[Enchanted Book Bundle]]|Vicious III ([[Vicious]])|5|Darker Auction ([[Dark Auction]]) when [[Scorpius]] is mayor|

The following Enchanted Book Bundles previously existed but have since been removed from the game. Existing copies are now blank Enchanted Book Bundles.

The following Enchanted Book Bundles exist in the game but are unobtainable. The Enchanted Books are directly given out instead.

|[[Enchanted Book Bundle]]|Transylvanian IV ([[Transylvanian]])|1|
## Usage
When right-clicked in the player's hand, they are given the listed quantity of the [[Enchanted Book|Enchanted Books]] the bundle contains. The bundle is consumed in the process.
## Bugs
- Opening an Enchanted Book Bundle in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] will cause the item to get wiped.

Certain brewing ingredients can be added during the brewing process to modify the potion. Other mechanics can also extend the length of the potion.

|[[Enchanted Glowstone]]|+3 levels|

|[[Potion Affinity Ring]]|+25% Duration|
|[[Potion Affinity Artifact]]|+50% Duration|
|[[Gunpowder]]|Duration ÷ 2; Splash|
|[[Enchanted Gunpowder]]|Splash|
|[[Parrot Pet]]|+40% Duration|
|[[Alchemy]]|+𝑥 Duration|
A [[Bitter Iced Tea]] can be added as the starting ingredient instead of a [[Awkward Potion]] to increase the final values of the potion by +20%. A [[Viking's Tear]] can be used instead to grant +20% experience orbs and +10 [[Combat Wisdom]].

# Flint Arrow
Categories: Items, Mob Drops, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Bows, Arrow Poisons, Arrows
[Flint Arrow]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Gregory]] [[Jax]] [[Ophelia]] [[Weaponsmith]]
Buy: 5 [[Coins]]
Sell: 2 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ARROW
Tooltip: Damage: +1; Stats added when shot!; COMMON ARROW
A Flint Arrow is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Arrows|Arrow]]. It grants +1 [[Damage]]. They can be purchased from several merchants, and can drop from [[Seer|Seers]].
## Obtaining
Flint Arrows can be obtained by killing mobs, purchasing from [[Shops|Merchants]], or [[Crafting]].
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv55]|[[Seer]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv55]|[[Seer]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv55]|[[Seer]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv55]|[[Seer]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
### Purchase
|Merchant|Price ([[Coins]]) 1x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 8x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 16x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 32x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 64x|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Weaponsmith]]|5 [[Coins]]​|40 [[Coins]]​|80 [[Coins]]​|160 [[Coins]]​|320 [[Coins]]|
|Merchant|Price ([[Coins]]) 1x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 8x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 16x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 32x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 64x|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ophelia]]|5 [[Coins]]​|40 [[Coins]]​|80 [[Coins]]​|160 [[Coins]]​|320 [[Coins]]|
|Merchant|Price ([[Coins]]) 64x|
|-|-|
|[[Jax]]|320 [[Coins]]|
|Merchant|Price ([[Coins]]) 20x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 40x​|Price ([[Coins]]) 60x|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Gregory]]|240 [[Coins]]​|480 [[Coins]]​|720 [[Coins]]|
Arrows have no daily purchase limit.
[[Gregory]] and [[Ophelia]] both sell arrows. They exist as a convenience option, both being located in areas players may urgently be in need of arrows. However, they are more expensive when bought from them instead of buying arrows in the hub.
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Stick]] 1x [[Feather]] 1x [[Flint]]|Recipe: [[Flint]] + [[Stick]] + [[Feather]] → Flint Arrow x4|
## Usage
### Quiver
Flint Arrows obtained either from the ground or through the [[Auto-pickup]] feature are automatically placed into the [[Quiver]], assuming space availability, prior to being added to the player's inventory. Players can manually transfer Flint Arrows into or out of the Quiver.
When using a [[Bows|Bow]], Flint Arrows located in the player's inventory are used before those in the Quiver. If there are no Flint Arrows in the inventory, the [[SkyBlock Menu]] transforms into a special ”Quiver Arrow" when the player is holding a [[Bows|Bow]]. This allows the player to use arrows from the Quiver. The Quiver Arrow is presented as a fixed stack of 64, irrespective of the actual quantity of arrows in the [[Quiver]]. Reverting to any item other than a Bow restores the SkyBlock Menu.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Voodoo Doll]]|Zombie Slayer V (5) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Crystallized Heart]] 1x Pumpkin Minion IX ([[Pumpkin Minion]]) 2x [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] 32x [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] [[Flint Arrow]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Flint Arrow|Recipe: Flint Arrow x64 ×4 + [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] x32 + [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] ×2 + [[Pumpkin Minion]] + [[Crystallized Heart]] → [[Voodoo Doll]]|
## Trivia
- [[Auto-pickup|Auto Pickup]] does not automatically fill the [[Quiver]] if Flint Arrows are collected from [[Chest|Chests]] broken on the [[Private Island]].

Tooltip: Click a stranger while holding to gift!; Both players get the rewards!; COMMON
Gifts are special items that can be opened. This is done by right clicking another player and having the other player right-click the placed gift. When opening, both players will receive one of the same rewards from that gift.
## Obtaining
All Gifts are purchasable at the [[Bazaar]], can be mined out of the walls of the [[Glacial Cave]] (excluding Party Gift), and are also rewards for successfully guarding the Gift Piles during [[Defend Jerry's Workshop]].
### [[White Gift|White Gifts]] ([[Rarity|COMMON]])
- 20 can be found hidden around the map of [[Jerry's Workshop]].
- As drops from [[Frozen Steve]], [[Frosty (mob)|Frosty]], [[Grinch]] and [[Reindrake]].
- One can be obtained every SkyBlock Year from [[St. Jerry]] by having him as an [[Abiphones|Abiphone contact]] and calling him.
### [[Green Gift|Green Gifts]] ([[Rarity|UNCOMMON]])
- One is obtained from [[St. Jerry]] as a reward after collecting the 20 white gifts at [[Jerry's Workshop]].
- As a drop from [[Grinch]] and [[Reindrake]].
### [[Red Gift|Red Gifts]] ([[Rarity|RARE]])
- From completing the first section of the [[Chicken Race]] (Obtainable once per [[Profile]]).
- Can be generated by [[Krampus Helmet]] as an upgrade item for minions.
- As a drop from [[Frosty (mob)|Frosty]], [[Grinch]], [[Yeti]], [[Nutcracker]] and [[Reindrake]].
### [[Party Gift]]
Party Gifts can only be obtained while [[Foxy]]'s A Time For Giving perk is active.
- As a drop from any [[Sea Creatures|Winter Sea Creatures]].

## Tips
- [[Snowman Mask]], [[White Gift Talisman]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] line, [[Snowfin Shard]] and the Professional Gifter perk from [[Doug]] give more rewards from Gifts.
- [[Snow Suit Armor|Snow Suit]] (including equipment) and [[Nutcracker Armor]], [[Reindeer Pet]], [[Hideongift Shard]], the Season of Joy perk from [[Essence Shops|Ice Essence Shop]] and the Present Connoisseur perk from [[Doug]] grant more Gifts from the [[Season of Jerry]] event.
## Trivia
- When a "Santa" rarity gift drop is received, it will be announced in the public chat as a "SANTA GIFT!"
- The [[Golden Gift]], even though it is named "gift," cannot be opened. It can only be used to purchase the [[Gold Gift Talisman]].
- On [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] mode, the player can only receive gifts from other Ironman players (and Bingo players), but they can give gifts to any player except Bingo players.
  - Similarly, on a [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profile, receiving gifts is only possible from other Bingo profiles, but gifts may be given to any other player.
- Attempting to send a gift to a mob will make the chat say 'Click a player to gift them! This isn't a player!'

## Usage
### Minion Fuel
Inferno Minion Fuel can be placed in [[Inferno Minion|Inferno Minions]] only. It lasts for 24 hours.
The specialty and Rarity of an Inferno Minion Fuel is determined by the [[Distillates|Distillate]] and tier of [[Gabagool (disambiguation)|Gabagool]] used in its crafting recipe. The Distillate ( [[Distillates|Magma Cream Distillate]], [[Distillates|Blaze Rod Distillate]], [[Distillates|Nether Wart Distillate]], [[Distillates|Glowstone Distillate]], or [[Distillates|Gabagool Distillate]]) used determines whether the Fuel has a specialty of [[Magma Cream]], [[Blaze Rod]], [[Nether Wart]], [[Glowstone Dust]], or [[Crude Gabagool]]. The specialty item will be collected by the Minion 80% of time it makes a harvest. The tier of Gabagool ( [[Fuel Gabagool]], [[Heavy Gabagool]] or [[Hypergolic Gabagool]]) used determines the speed boost of the fuel, with [[Rarity|RARE]] giving 10x, [[Rarity|EPIC]] giving 15x, and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] giving 20x speed.
### [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Fuel drops
[[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Fuel also gives the Minion a chance to produce [[Chili Pepper|Chili Peppers]], [[Inferno Vertex|Inferno Vertices]], [[Inferno Apex|Inferno Apexes]], [[Reaper Pepper|Reaper Peppers]], and [[Gabagool the Fish]]. For every harvesting action by the Minion, each rare drop is rolled, and can be generated by the minion.
Drop Chances (per roll):
- Chili Pepper: 0.7352941176% (1 in 136)
- Inferno Vertex: 0.0168067227% (1 in 5,950)
- Inferno Apex: 0.0000763889% (1 in 1,309,091)
- Reaper Pepper: 0.0002182539% (1 in 458,182)
- Gabagool the Fish: 0.0000254629% (1 in 3,927,273)
After expiring, [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] fuel produces 1 [[Hypergolic Ionized Ceramics]].
## Bugs
- The speed multipliers are applied along with the other speed buffs to the base action time as follows:baseActionTime(1+speedBuffs)*(1+InfernoMinionFuelMultiplier). This extra 1+ in front of the Inferno Minion Fuel multiplier causes the effective multipliers to be 11x, 16x, and 21x.

|Aug 5, 2022|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) Inferno Minion Fuel now produces rare drops 10% more often.|

# Kuudra Teeth Plaque
Aliases: Decaying Kuudra Teeth Plaque, Exquisite Kuudra Teeth Plaque, Gnarly Kuudra Teeth Plaque, Plaque, Repulsive Kuudra Teeth Plaque, Unpleasant Kuudra Teeth Plaque
Categories: Items
[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]), EPIC ([[Rarity]]), RARE ([[Rarity]]), UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]), COMMON ([[Rarity]]) and SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: KUUDRA_CAVITY
Tooltip: A bacteria proven to decay teeth. This bacteria, however, seems of the highest quality.; Maybe you can find a way to break it down!; * Soulbound * LEGENDARY
The Kuudra Teeth Plaque is a series of items required to obtain the [[Kuudra Follower Relic]] and [[Kuudra Mandible]].
## Obtaining
The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Exquisite Kuudra Teeth Plaque can be obtained from [[Drakuu]] when clicking them with a [[Golden Ball]] while wearing [[Kuudra Follower Armor]].
Afterwards, the [[Rarity]] of the plaque can be decreased down to [[Rarity|COMMON]] by completing the following steps in any order:
- Talk to the [[Kuudra Archeologist]] with at least 4,500 [[Intelligence]] (4,600 [[Mana|Maximum Mana]]).
- Talk to [[Arch]] with a [Lv100] [[Kuudra Pet]] equipped.
- Talk to the [[Kuudra Believer]] and enter "decay" into the donation prompt.
- Type the line "What just happened!? Is this Kuudra's real lair?" to [[Kuudra Loremaster]] after speaking with them.
## Usage
The [[Rarity|COMMON]] Decaying Kuudra Teeth Plaque can presented to [[Crag]] while wearing full [[Kuudra Follower Armor]] and holding the [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]]. By doing this, the following will occur:
- The plaque will be turned into a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] Kuudra Teeth Plaque, and add an edition number to it.
- The [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]] will be turned into the [[Kuudra Follower Relic]].
- The player will unlock the ability to obtain the [[Kuudra Mandible]] from [[Kuudra|Kuudra Loot]].
## Trivia
- This item comes in more rarities than any other, with the amount being 6; and an additional two if it is recombobulated at the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] and the [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] variants.
## History

|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added the Repulsive Kuudra Teeth Plaque.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added the Decaying Kuudra Teeth Plaque.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added the Kuudra Teeth Plaque.|
|May 11, 2023|0.18.4|Fixed grammar in the Kuudra Teeth Plaque.|

# Lanterns
Categories: Items, Pages missing IDs
[Lanterns]
Type: [[Deployables]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tooltip: Mining Deployable; Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK Place an orb for 5m buffing up to 5; players within 30 blocks.; Costs 50% of max mana.; Orb Buff: Dwarven Lantern ▪ Grants +20 Mining Speed; Only one deployable buff applies.; COMMON DEPLOYABLE
Lanterns are a sub-class of [[Deployables]].
## Mechanics
Lanterns have no cooldown, cost 50% of the players max mana and buffs mining stats.
### Upgrading
|Slot|Upgrade|Effect|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Mana Disintegrator]]|[[Mana Disintegrator]]|Can be combined in an [[Anvil]] with a Lantern to reduce their [[Mana]] cost by 1%.|A [[Mana Disintegrator]] may be applied up to 10 times!|

# Loudmouth Bass
Aliases: Bass, Loudmouth
Categories: Items
[Loudmouth Bass]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])-DIVINE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LOUDMOUTH_BASS
Tooltip: Right-click a player holding a Loudmouth Bass to engage in a duel!; The heavier your bass is, the more likely it is to win!; The winning bass will consume the other and grow heavier.; If your bass is consumed, you receive some Fish Food and Social XP as compensation, scaling with bass weight.; Current weight: 1 lb Upgrades rarity in 49 lbs; COMMON
The Loudmouth Bass is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
### Seal's Treat Bag
A Loudmouth Bass has a 11.1% (1 in 9) chance to be obtained from a [[Seal's Treat Bag]].
### Raffle of the Century
A Loudmouth Bass can also be obtained as a Minor Reward during [[Raffle of the Century]] event, or from the [[Gift Bag of the Century]].
## Usage
A Loudmouth Bass can be used to challenge another player holding one to a duel. The winner's Loudmouth Bass will gain weight, while the losing party will lose their Loudmouth Bass.
The Loudmouth Bass upgrades its rarity depending on its weight:
|Rarity|Weight|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|0-49 lb|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|50-2,499 lb|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|2,500-9,999 lb|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|10,000-24,999 lb|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|25,000-49,999 lb|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|50,000-99,999 lb|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|100,000+ lb|
The losing party is rewarded with 1 [[Fish Food]], and 1 [[Social|Social XP]] for every 10 lb their Loudmouth Bass had, with a minimum of 100 and a maximum of 500,000 [[Social|Social XP]].
The XP gained can be calculated using the following formula:
SocialXPGained=max⁡(100,min⁡(⌊LoserBassWeight10⌋,500000))
For example: If the losing player's Loudmouth Bass weight is between 0 and 1,000 lb, then they will always get 100 [[Social|Social XP]]. If the weight was 1,010 lb, they will get 101 [[Social|Social XP]].
If losing player's Loudmouth Bass weight was more than 50 lb, they will also get a [[Skeleton the Fish]].
### Calculator
calcpage  = Calculator:Loudmouth Bass
 template  = Template:Calculator/Loudmouth_Bass
 form      = loudmouthBassCalcForm
 calcname  = Loudmouth Bass Calculator
 result    = inner

 param     = weight|Loser bass mass|0|number
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
### Odexar
Loudmouth Bass with a weight of at least 101 lbs can be shown to [[Odexar]] to show unique dialogue.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Winning a duel|CHOMP! Your Loudmouth Bass emerged victorious and grew to <new mass> lbs! (+<losing bass mass> lbs)|
|Losing a duel|CHOMP! Your <losing bass mass> lbs Loudmouth Bass was eaten! FEED ME MORE! There's nothing left but bones...|
|Losing a duel|CHOMP! Your <losing bass mass> lbs Loudmouth Bass was eaten! Not even close, baby! There's nothing left but bones...|
|Losing a duel|CHOMP! Your <losing bass mass> lbs Loudmouth Bass was eaten! Lil' bro's gone in a single bite! There's nothing left but bones...|
|Losing a duel|CHOMP! Your <losing bass mass> lbs Loudmouth Bass was eaten! gg ez There's nothing left but bones...|
|Losing a duel|CHOMP! Your <losing bass mass> lbs Loudmouth Bass was eaten! Gonna cry for your bass back? There's nothing left but bones...|
|Losing a duel|CHOMP! Your <losing bass mass> lbs Loudmouth Bass was eaten! This wasn't even half a challenge! There's nothing left but bones...|
|Losing a duel|CHOMP! Your <losing bass mass> lbs Loudmouth Bass was eaten! Too easy! Try again in 100 year's time! There's nothing left but bones...|
|Losing a duel|CHOMP! Your <losing bass mass> lbs Loudmouth Bass was eaten! Looks like I'm a pescatarian! There's nothing left but bones...|
|Sending a duel request|Sent a Loudmouth Bass duel request to <rank> <name>!|
|Sending a duel request failed Opponent is already challenged|This player is already currently challenged by someone else to a Loudmouth Bass Duel!|
|Sending a duel request failed Either of the players is no longer holding their bass|Someone is no longer holding their bass, so the duel was cancelled.|
|Sending a duel request failed Attempted challengee is not holding a Loudmouth Bass|This player is not holding a Loudmouth Bass!|
## Trivia
- A Loudmouth Bass cannot be recombobulated.
- The maximum possible weight a Loudmouth Bass can have is 4,000,000,000,000,000 lbs, or 4 quadrillion. After reaching this weight, its weight will not increase further.
  - Despite it not increasing further beyond this point, chat feedback will say it does. This does not reflect in the item's description nor NBT data.
- It would take 900,009 (14,062 stacks plus 41) [[Seal's Treat Bag]], worth , to reach [[Rarity|DIVINE]] rarity.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 28, 2025|Added Loudmouth Bass.|
|Mar 18, 2025|0.22|Added Loudmouth Bass to [[Seal's Treat Bag]].|
|Oct 15, 2025|0.23.5|Changed [[Skeleton the Fish]] requirement from 1,000 lb to 50 lb.|

# Magical Map
Aliases: Dungeon Map, Dungeon map
Categories: Items, Pages with non-auctionable items with valid auctionable item IDs, Outdated pages, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Magical Map]
Type: Dungeon ([[Dungeons]]) Control Item
Tooltip Text: Shows the layout of the Dungeon as it is explored and completed.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MAP
Tooltip: Shows the layout of the Dungeon as it is explored and completed.
The Magical Map is an item that was added in the [[Dungeons|Dungeons Update]]. you receive one at the start of each dungeon run, replacing the [[SkyBlock Menu]] (You can still access it by right-clicking the map.).
Its current use is to show what rooms you have cleared, as well as connected rooms you have not. It also shows where all the member of your [[Dungeons]] party are.
After the final boss of the floor is defeated, the map will turn into a map that depict your stats and score during this dungeon run. It will then return to becoming the SkyBlock menu after traveling out of this lobby.
## Magical Map Locations
This article needs to be reworked as its content is outdated. You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
This article was last edited by Mrxp (contribs|logs|block) on 5:33, October 13 2026 (13 days ago)
The following dot-points describe each location found on the magical map. It should also be noted that if the required monsters in a room are defeated, a white check mark will be placed over it. If a puzzle room is failed a red cross will appear. If a white check mark turns green, it is an indication that all secrets in that room have been found.
- Green Room: The Green Room is where each Run begins.
- Brown Rooms: Brown Rooms contain the most secrets for exploration, and contain normal mobs and sometimes a miniboss, which is often not as difficult to kill as the yellow room mini-bosses.
- Yellow Room ([[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] Room): Contains a floor specific [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that is more difficult to beat than other [[Minibosses]] found in the dungeon (except the watcher)
- Purple Rooms ([[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms|Puzzle Rooms]]): Contains a puzzle of a random selection, Different Floors have different puzzles.
- Orange Room (Trap Rooms): Found on Floor 3 and above, this room is filled with traps and levers that must be found to reveal a chest at the end which can clear this floor
- Pink Room: Fairy Room which can be used to heal in the pool, or kill fairies for revive stone.
- Red Room (Blood Room): The final [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] room containing [[The Watcher]], with increasing difficulty on each consecutive floor. This will need to be cleared to get access to the [[Bosses|BOSS]] room.
  - In [[The Catacombs - Entrance]], the floor will be cleared after defeating The Watcher and [[Dungeon Score]] will be calculated instantly.
- Question Mark Rooms: These rooms are unexplored, if at the door it contains a solid black line it usually indicated that there is a wither door (not always) and if there is a red line at the door of this room it indicated that this is the final mini-boss room.
## Trivia
- The Magical Map can sometimes glitch, causing it to freeze and not update a player's location.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Magical Map.|

# Mixins
Aliases: Ender Portal Fumes, Mixin
Categories: Items, Pages with non-auctionable items with valid auctionable item IDs, Brewing, Potions, Slayer
[Mixins]
Type: Mixin
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ZOMBIE_BRAIN_MIXIN; SPIDER_EGG_MIXIN; WOLF_FUR_MIXIN; END_PORTAL_FUMES_MIXIN; GABAGOEY_MIXIN; DEEPTERROR_MIXIN; MUSHED_MUSHROOM_MIXIN; HOT_CHOCOLATE_MIXIN; BLENDED_FISH_MIXIN; PIG_BRAIN_MIXIN; MELON_JUICE_MIXIN; HOTSPOT_TONIC_MIXIN; CELESTIAL_MASON_JAR; SCATHA_IN_A_BOTTLE; BLIZZARD_BOTTLE
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Gain +10 Ferocity!; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); ☠ Requires Zombie Slayer 8.; RARE
Mixins are [[Rarity|RARE]] or [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] items that can be brewed together with a [[God Potion]] or consumed to grant an additional effect for its duration.

## Usage
### Brewing
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: [[Zombie Brain Mixin]]/[[Spider Egg Mixin]]/[[Wolf Fur Mixin]]/[[End Portal Fumes]]/[[Gabagoey Mixin]]/[[Deepterror Mixin]]/[[Glowing Mush Mixin]]/[[Hot Chocolate Mixin]]/[[Blended Fish Mixin]]/[[Pig Brain Mixin]] → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] and [[God Potion]] are both active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## Trivia
- Even if the player does not meet the requirements, they can still combine a Mixin with a [[God Potion]] in a [[Brewing Stand]].
  - However, they still will be unable to consume the God Potion.
- Any number of Mixins can be applied to the same God Potion. Their order in the tooltip is the same order in which they were applied. Example of a maxed [[God Potion]]:
  - To purchase all the necessary mixins for this, it would cost a total of . However, since mixins can be brewed into up to three God Potions at once, the effective cost is only per God Potion.
- Despite the Mixin's name being [[Pig Brain Mixin]] the effect shows as "Pigs Brain" on the God Potion tooltip.
- All Mixins have a hidden cooldown of 0.5s.

# Music Discs
Aliases: Disc, Discs, Music Disc, Record, Records
Categories: Items
[Music Discs]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]]), EPIC ([[Rarity]]), LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes

Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DUNGEON_DISC_1; DUNGEON_DISC_2; DUNGEON_DISC_3; DUNGEON_DISC_4; DUNGEON_DISC_5; SPOOKY_DISC; BATTLE_DISC; WINTER_DISC
Tooltip: Place this inside a jukebox to play a dungeon song!; Dungeon Drama By ShinkoNet; EPIC
Custom Music Discs are items that will play a song if placed into a [[Jukebox]] obtained through various methods.
## Obtaining
The [[Spooky Disc]] is obtained by placing in the top 2000 players during a [[Spooky Festival]].
The [[Winter Disc]] and [[Battle Disc|Battle Discs]] are obtained from [[Gifts]].
The Dungeon Discs are obtained from Post-Boss Reward Chests.
## Types
### Dungeon Discs
The following discs are obtained through Dungeons, and play Dungeon tracks. [[Old Disc]] is the only [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] disc in the game currently. All the other Dungeon Discs are [[Rarity|EPIC]].
|Icon|Name|Track|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dungeon Disc]]|[[Dungeon Disc]]|"Dungeon Drama ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"; By ShinkoNet|Rare drop from from Obsidian or Bedrock Reward Chest on all [[Catacombs]] floors|
|[[Clown Disc]]|[[Clown Disc]]|"Superior Judgement ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"; By SirCatcelot|(same as above)|
|[[Watcher Disc]]|[[Watcher Disc]]|"The Watcher ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"; By ShinkoNet|(same as above)|
|[[Necron Disc]]|[[Necron Disc]]|"Necron Doom ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"; By ShinkoNet|(same as above)|
|[[Old Disc]]|[[Old Disc]]|"The Wither King ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"; By ShinkoNet|Rare drop from Obsidian or Bedrock Reward Chest on all Master Mode [[Catacombs]] floors|
### Event Discs
The following discs are obtained through events.
|Icon|Name|Track|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Spooky Disc]]|[[Spooky Disc]]|"Hallow Hallow ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"; By ShinkoNet|[[Spooky Festival]]; Place within the top 2000 in the event|
|[[Battle Disc]]|[[Battle Disc]]|"blastin banter battle ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"; By ShinkoNet|[[Season of Jerry]]; Obtained from gifts|
|[[Winter Disc]]|[[Winter Disc]]|"Let Them Eat Cake ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"; By ShinkoNet|[[Season of Jerry]]; Obtained from gifts|
## See Also
- [[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]]
## Trivia
- If you leave it playing and leave your island, then return, the disc would have stopped playing, but is still in the jukebox.
- No matter how far you are from the jukebox that is playing the disc, you will hear it at the same volume across your island.
- The track of [[Battle Disc]] is referred to as "Blast in Banter Battle" in the item's tooltip, but "blastin banter battle" on its Youtube page.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 24, 2019|0.7.3|Added [[Spooky Disc]].|
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Added [[Battle Disc]] and [[Winter Disc]].|
|Mar 1, 2022|0.12.3|Added [[Dungeon Disc]], [[Clown Disc]], [[Watcher Disc]], [[Old Disc]] and [[Necron Disc]].|

# Power Orbs
Aliases: Orbs
Categories: Items, Pages missing IDs
[Power Orbs]
Type: [[Deployables]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tooltip: Ability: Deploy RIGHT CLICK Place an orb for 1m buffing up to 5; players within 18 blocks.; Mana Cost: 50% of max Vitality Cost: 50; Orb Buff: Radiant • Heal yourself for 30❤ per second.; • Heal others for 15❤ per second.; • Gain +25 Health Regen.; Only one deployable buff applies.; ☠ Requires Wolf Slayer 2.; UNCOMMON DEPLOYABLE
Power Orbs are a sub-class of [[Deployables]].
## Mechanics
Power Orbs have no cooldown, have shorter range, and buff less players than [[Flares]], but actively heal players when placed.
### Upgrading
|Slot|Upgrade|Effect|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Recombobulator 3000]]|[[Recombobulator 3000]]|Can be combined in an [[Anvil]] with a Power Orb to permanently increase the [[Rarity]] by +1.|An item's rarity can only be upgraded once!|
|[[Mana Disintegrator]]|[[Mana Disintegrator]]|Can be combined in an [[Anvil]] with a Power Orb to reduce their [[Mana]] cost by 1%.|A [[Mana Disintegrator]] may be applied up to 10 times!|
|[[Jalapeno Book]]|[[Jalapeno Book]]|Can be combined in an [[Anvil]] with a Power Orb to gain a small but permanent stat boost of +5 [[Crit Damage]] and +1% [[Crit Chance]].|A [[Jalapeno Book]] may only be applied once!|
## Power Orbs
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Effect|Source|

# Power Stones
Aliases: Power Stone, Powerstone
Categories: Items, Power Stones
[Power Stones]
Type: Item
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ACACIA_BIRDHOUSE; BEATING_HEART; BUBBA_BLISTER; DARK_ORB; DISPLACED_LEECH; ECCENTRIC_PAINTING; END_STONE_SHULKER; ENDER_MONOCLE; CHOCOLATE_CHIP; FURBALL; GLACITE_SHARD; HAZMAT_ENDERMAN; HORNS_OF_TORMENT; LUXURIOUS_SPOOL; MAGMA_URCHIN; MANDRAA; OBSIDIAN_TABLET; PRECIOUS_PEARL; ROCK_CANDY; SCORCHED_BOOKS; SUNFLOWER_BUTTER; VITAMIN_DEATH
Tooltip: Power Stone Combine x9 of this item at the Thaumaturgist in the Hub to unlock the Forceful power.; At 1,000 Magical Power:; +54.28&#xE010; Health +581.62&#xE00D; Strength +155.1&#xE007; Crit Damage; Unique Power Bonus:; +4&#xE00B; Ferocity; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 20.; RARE POWER STONE
Power Stones are items that can be used to unlock new [[Powers|Accessory Powers]].
## Mechanics
Power Stones are used to unlock Accessory Powers that are not obtainable by default. 9x of the same Power Stone can be given to [[Maxwell]] to combine them, unlocking a new Power that can be applied to the player's [[Accessory Bag]]. Each Power gives a unique stat boost while that power is in use, although this bonus is not increased as accessory power is increased.

## Trivia
- Some Powers inherit their names from old [[Accessories|Accessory]] [[Reforging|Reforges]], and some even have the names of which the stones used to provide (Shaded, Sweet, Silky, Bloody).
- There are no Power Stones with [[Rarity|COMMON]] rarity.

# Prehistoric Egg
Categories: Items, Crystal Hollows, Pets, Mining pets
[Prehistoric Egg]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])-LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: "After walking a certain amount of blocks, has a chance to spawn an Armadillo ([[Armadillo Pet]])!"
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PREHISTORIC_EGG
Tooltip: After walking a certain amount of blocks, has a chance to spawn an Armadillo!; 4,000: 50% for a Common Armadillo!; 10,000: 50% for an Uncommon Armadillo!; 20,000: 50% for a Rare Armadillo!; 40,000: 50% for an Epic Armadillo!; 100,000: 100% for a Legendary Armadillo!; COMMON
A Prehistoric Egg is an egg that hatches into the [[Armadillo Pet]] after walking a certain amount of blocks.
## Obtaining
Prehistoric Eggs can be obtained from treasure chests in the [[Crystal Hollows]] as well as from the [[Crystal Nucleus]].
## Usage
The Prehistoric Egg hatches into an [[Armadillo Pet]] after walking a certain amount of blocks on all islands. Moving in other ways does count towards this (for example by using a [[Grappling Hook]], by flying and teleporting, though teleporting only counts as moving one block), except when riding on a [[Horse Pet]] or [[Armadillo Pet]]. The Egg's rarity increases based on what [[Armadillo Pet]] can be hatched from it.
|Steps|Rarity|Chance|Overall chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|4,000|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|50%|50%|
|10,000|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|50%|25%|
|20,000|RARE ([[Rarity]])|50%|12.5%|
|40,000|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|50%|6.25%|
|100,000|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|100%|6.25%|
When failing to hatch at the above distances, the Prehistoric Egg will cross out that line of text and update its rarity to its new potential hatch.
## Trivia
- [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulating]] this item will not increase its rarity, but it will add the usual obfuscated text indicating it has been recombobulated.
- The Prehistoric Egg has an average profit of .

The Rift Necklace is a [[Necklaces|Necklace]]. It has two versions: one inside the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]], and another outside.
## Obtaining
### Elise
A [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Rift Necklace can be claimed from [[Elise (Rift)|Elise]] inside the [[Rift Dimension]] after placing a [[Supreme Timecharm]] in the [[Rift Gallery]]. Once the player has claimed their necklace inside the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]], they can talk to [[Elise]] in the [[Wizard Tower]] and selecting the keyword CARAMEL to receive a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Rift Necklace.

- Outside of specific activities, the Rift Necklace is the best-in-slot necklace obtainable. It should be obtained and upgraded early on as it will remain useful.

# Roofed Forest Island
Categories: Items, Craftable items, Dark Oak Log Collection, Spawnable islands, Blocks, Foraging
[Roofed Forest Island]
Type: Island Spawner
Collection ([[Collections]]): Dark Oak Log IV (4) ([[Dark Oak Log]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 30x [[Dirt]] 15x [[Dark Oak Log]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 30 [[Dirt]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ROOFED_FOREST_ISLAND
Tooltip: Place this in your private world to spawn a Roofed Forest island!; COMMON
The Roofed Forest Island is a [[Spawnable Islands|Spawnable Island]] unlocked at [[Dark Oak Log|Dark Oak Log IV (4)]].
Placing it creates an Island composed of [[Dirt]], [[Coarse Dirt]], [[Mushroom|Red Mushrooms]], [[Mushroom|Brown Mushrooms]], [[Leaves|Dark Oak Leaves]], [[Stone]], [[Andesite]], [[Long Grass]], [[Torch|Torches]], [[Wooden Fence|Dark Oak Fences]], [[Grass|Grass Blocks]], and [[Dark Oak Log]].
It also contains a [[Floating Crystals|Bat Crystal]], which grants a chance for [[Bat (disambiguation)|Bats]] to spawn, with an effective radius of 20.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Dark Oak Log IV (4) ([[Dark Oak Log]])|15x [[Dark Oak Log]] 30x [[Dirt]]|Recipe: [[Dark Oak Log]] x5 ×3 + [[Dirt]] x5 ×6 → Roofed Forest Island|
## Island Contents
A Roofed Forest Island contains the following:
- 3,719 (58 stacks plus 7) [[Stone]]
- 1,527 (23 stacks plus 55) [[Dirt]]
- 1,499 (23 stacks plus 27) [[Leaves|Dark Oak Leaves]]
- 535 (8 stacks plus 23) [[Dark Oak Log]]
- 62 [[Coarse Dirt]]
- 75 (1 stack plus 11) [[Andesite]]
- 33 [[Long Grass]]
- 4 [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]]
- 3 [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]]
- 2 [[Torch]]
- 2 [[Wooden Fence|Dark Oak Fence]]
## Trivia
- The [[Leaves|Dark Oak Leaves]] and [[Dark Oak Log]] are replenished by the [[Floating Crystals|Bat Crystal]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Roofed Forest Island.|
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Changed the collection requirement from Dark Oak Log VI (6) ([[Dark Oak Log]]) to Dark Oak Log IV (4) ([[Dark Oak Log]]).|

# SkyBlock Menu
Aliases: Menu, Skyblock Menu
Categories: Items, Pages containing missing tooltips, Pages with UIs, Mechanics, SkyBlock Menu
[SkyBlock Menu]
Type: Item
Source: Logging in on SkyBlock
Uses: Accessing Menu
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SKYBLOCK_MENU
Tooltip: View all of your SkyBlock progress, including your Skills, Collections, Recipes, and more!; Click to open!
The SkyBlock Menu is the main Hypixel SkyBlock menu. You can access your general SkyBlock info, your SkyBlock profile, SkyBlock settings, and the Hypixel Menu. The item is located on the last slot of your hotbar.
## How to open
To access the SkyBlock Menu, either:
- Right-click or left-click the item when it's in your main hand.
- Open your inventory, then click on the item.
- Open chat and run the command /sbmenu.
- Drop the item in any form.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Buttons
Note: The bottom-left button has the location ID of (1, 1) and the top-right button has location ID of (9, 6).
|Button|Function|Location (The location in the menu. The first coordinate is the horizontal location, while the second one is vertical location.)|Unlocking|
|-|-|-|-|
|Stats & Equipment|Opens your [[Equipment Menu]].|5; 5||
|Your Skills ([[Skills]])|Opens the Skills Menu.|2; 4||
|[[Collections]]|Opens the Collections Menu.|3; 4||
|[[Recipe Book]]|Opens the Recipe Book.|4; 4||
|Skyblock Leveling ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|Opens the SkyBlock Leveling ([[SkyBlock Levels]]) menu.|5; 4||
|Quest & Chapters ([[Quests & Chapters]])|Opens the [[Quests & Chapters]] menu.|6; 4||
|Calendar and Events ([[Calendar]])|Opens the calendar. Also displays the nearest event.|7; 4||
|[[Storage]]|Opens the Storage.|8; 4||
|Your Bags|Opens Your Bags menu.|3; 3||
|[[Pets]]|Opens the Pets menu.|4; 3|Unlocks automatically when player obtains their first pet.|
|[[Crafting Table]]|Opens the crafting grid.|5; 3||
|[[Wardrobe]]|Opens your Wardrobe.|6; 3|Combat V (5) ([[Combat]])|
|[[Personal Bank]]|Opens the Bank menu.|7; 3|Emerald VI (6) ([[Emerald]])|
|[[Fast Travel]]|Opens the Fast Travel menu.|3; 1|Unlocked after entering the Hub Portal for the first time.|
|Profile Management ([[Profile]])|Shows the profile management menu.|4; 1||
|Close|Close the Skyblock Menu.|5; 1||
|[[Settings]]|Opens the Settings Menu.|6; 1||
|[[Booster Cookie]]|Opens the Booster Cookie Menu. Only available when you have an active Booster Cookie.|7; 1||
## Tips
- As some of your stats such as [[Health]] or [[Crit Chance]] might change when holding a certain item, you can check your stats with the item on your hand by accessing Your SkyBlock Profile in your SkyBlock Menu by selecting it in your inventory, or via using the command /sbmenu with the item in your hand.
## History

# Time Shuriken
Categories: Items
[Time Shuriken]
Type: Sword ([[Swords]])
Source: Sun Gecko Fight ([[Time Chamber]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TIME_KNIFE
Tooltip: Can be thrown at Sun Gecko to upkeep your combo.; Why are you reading this? Go fight Sun Gecko!; * Soulbound * SPECIAL SWORD
Time Shuriken is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Swords|Sword]] used during the [[Sun Gecko]] fight.
## Obtaining
Time Shuriken can be obtained by picking them up during the [[Sun Gecko]] fight while the Revival modifier is active.
## Usage
Time Shuriken can be thrown at [[Sun Gecko]] during the fight to keep the player's current combo.
## Trivia
- [[Extremely Real Shuriken]] can be used instead of Time Shuriken to maintain the player's current combo.
- Although Time Shuriken is thrown like [[Extremely Real Shuriken]], its type differs, [[Swords|Sword]] instead of Item, which is consistent with its item ID (TIME_KNIFE).
- Time Shuriken is exclusive to the [[Sun Gecko]] fight, and will be removed from the player's inventory upon leaving the [[Time Chamber]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Time Shuriken Added|

# Travel Scrolls
Aliases: Fast Travel Scroll, Fast Travel Scrolls, MUSEUM TRAVEL SCROLL, Travel Scroll, Travel Scroll to, Travel Scroll to Blazing Fortress, Travel Scroll to Crimson Isle, Travel Scroll to Dragontail, Travel Scroll to Dwarven Base Camp, Travel Scroll to Forgotten Skull, Travel Scroll to Galatea, Travel Scroll to Safari Zone Entrance, Travel Scroll to Scarleton, Travel Scroll to Smoldering Tomb, Travel Scroll to The Wasteland, Travel Scroll to the Barn, Travel Scroll to the Blaze Farm, Travel Scroll to the Crystal Hollows, Travel Scroll to the Dwarven Mines, Travel Scroll to the Foraging Islands, Travel Scroll to the Kuudra Passage, Travel Scroll to the Murkwater Loch, Travel Scroll to the Park, Travel Scroll to the Wasteland, Travel scroll, Travel scroll to, Travel scrolls, Warp, Warp Scroll, Warp Scrolls
Categories: Items, Pages missing IDs, Travel Scrolls, Rare items, Epic items
[Travel Scrolls]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])-EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: Varies
Auctionable: Varies
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Tooltip: Consume this item to add its destination to your Fast Travel options.; Requires MVP+ to consume!; Island: Crimson Isle Teleport: Forgotten Skull; EPIC TRAVEL SCROLL
Travel Scrolls are items that unlock the ability to warp to certain [[Locations|Zones]] when consumed. The player can warp to a zone either via the [[Fast Travel]] menu or the [[Commands|/warp]] command. Travel Scrolls are similar to [[Portals]] in function, but allow players to warp directly instead of going through a portal on a [[Private Island]]. All Travel Scrolls, however, can be converted into Portals by using a [[Portalizer]] on them in an anvil.
Travel Scrolls come in two rarities: [[Rarity|RARE]] and [[Rarity|EPIC]]. The former can be used by all players, and the latter requires 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] or above to use.

## Trivia
- Bingo players have all Travel Scrolls unlocked regardless of their rank, including unobtainable ones.
- Travel Scroll to Dark Auction is referred to as "Travel Scroll to Sirius" in the Dark Auction.
- Despite the respective area being called [[Ruins]], the command /warp ruins doesn't work, unlike /warp castle.

Tooltip: Caught everywhere.; Bring this to Odger at the top of the Blazing Volcano.; * Soulbound * COMMON TROPHY
Trophy Fish are a series of [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]] that can be caught on the [[Crimson Isle]]. They come in four tiers, Bronze, Silver, Gold, and Diamond, and can be filleted by [[Odger]] for [[Magmafish]]. Collecting each quality of each variant of Trophy Fish grants rewards.
[[Trophy Chance]] increases the chance to catch Trophy Fish.
## Obtaining
Trophy Fish can be occasionally caught while fishing on the [[Crimson Isle]], with different requirements to catch each Trophy Fish. There are four tiers of each Trophy Fish: Bronze, Silver, Gold, and Diamond.
The [[Trophy Chance]] [[Stats|Stat]] can be used to increase the chance of Trophy Fish being fished up. The Midas Lure perk from [[Marigold]] and Radiant Fisher perk from [[Gemma]] only apply to Gold and Diamond [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]] respectively.
When the player catches something while [[Fishing]], all Trophy Fish that have their requirements met for are rolled as possible catches, starting from the lowest to highest catch chance.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] If no Trophy Fishes are caught, normal [[Fishing]] loot is rolled.
In case the player has not caught a Gold and/or Diamond tier of a Trophy Fish by then, after 100 and 600 catches of that Trophy Fish, respectively, the next Trophy Fish caught is guaranteed to be that specific type and tier due to [[Pity and RNG Meters|Pity]].
### Tier Chances
When a Trophy Fish is caught, the game rolls for which Trophy Fish and tier to give the player. The rarest Trophy Fish rolls first. If Trophy Fishes have the same chance, they are rolled in alphabetical order.
Once the Trophy Fish is chosen, the game attempts to roll the Diamond tier first. If the Diamond tier is not chosen, Gold is rolled next, and so on. If the first three tiers are not granted, Bronze is guaranteed. The [[Charm]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] applies to each of these chances, except Bronze.
- Diamond: 0.2% (0.224% with [[Charm|Charm VI]])
- Gold: 2% (2.24% with [[Charm|Charm VI]])
- Silver: 25% (28% with [[Charm|Charm VI]])
- Bronze: 100%
Overall, the chance for each tier is as follows. The values below are exact.
- Diamond: 0.2% (0.224% with [[Charm|Charm VI]])
- Gold: 1.996% (2.2349824% with [[Charm|Charm VI]])
- Silver: 24.451% (27.311484928% with [[Charm|Charm VI]])
- Bronze: 73.353% (70.229532672% with [[Charm|Charm VI]])
|Slot|Ability|Source|Percentage Bonus|GOLD Trophy Fishes|DIAMOND Trophy Fishes|All Trophies ([[Trophy (disambiguation)]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Marigold]]|Midas Lure|[[Marigold]]|2–20%|✔ Yes|✕ No|✔ Yes|
|[[Gemma]]|Radiant Fisher|[[Gemma]]|2–20%|✕ No|✔ Yes|✔ Yes|
|[[Spinosaurus Pet]]|Pursuit|[[Spinosaurus Pet]]|0.1–10%|✔ Yes|✔ Yes|✕ No|
|[[Barrel of Riches]]|[[Barrel of Riches]]|Crafting|10%|✔ Yes|✕ No|✔ Yes|
|[[Lotus Crown]]|[[Lotus Crown]]|Crafting|10%|✕ No|✔ Yes|✔ Yes|
|[[Golden Froggles]]|Hi-Vis Wormhole Helmet|[[Golden Froggles]]Lotus VII (7) ([[Lotus]])|5%|✔ Yes|✕ No|✔ Yes|
|[[Diamond Froggles]]|Hi-Vis Wormhole Helmet|[[Diamond Froggles]]Lotus XI (11) ([[Lotus]])|10% for GOLD; 5% for DIAMOND|✔ Yes|✔ Yes|✔ Yes|
With all buffs, the chances of diamond and gold trophy fish are as follows.
- GOLD:
  - 3.56971388928% when fishing in a [[Wormhole]] with [[Diamond Froggles]]
  - 3.2451944448% when not fishing in a [[Wormhole]]
- DIAMOND:
  - 0.3577728% when fishing in a [[Wormhole]] with [[Diamond Froggles]]
  - 0.325248% when not fishing in a [[Wormhole]]

## Usage
### Rewards
Catching individual tiers of Trophy Fishes can earn rewards from [[Odger]].
Additionally, catching Gold and Diamond Trophy Fishes yields 15 [[Bits]] and 30 [[Bits]] respectively, increased by the player's [[Bits]] multiplier.
### Filleting
Trophy Fish can be filleted by [[Odger]] to earn [[Magmafish]].

### SkyBlock Levels
Catching a Bronze, Silver, Gold and Diamond of one type of Trophy Fish for the first time grants 4, 8, 16 and 32 [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]] respectively.
Catching a higher tier also awards the [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]] of lower tiers.
### [[Achievements]]
A Trophy Fish can be put in the furnace near the [[Melancholic Viking]] to earn the [[Achievements|Meal fit for a King]] [[Achievements|Achievement]].
## Trivia
- The zones for [[Trophy Fish|Moldfin]], [[Trophy Fish|Volcanic Stonefish]], [[Trophy Fish|Flyfish]], and [[Trophy Fish|Skeleton Fish]] are slightly overlapped with their adjacent zones. For example, both [[Trophy Fish|Moldfin]] and [[Trophy Fish|Volcanic Stonefish]] can be caught at the west edge of [[Mystic Marsh]].

# Trophy Frogs
Aliases: Blessed Frog, Bullfrog, Cave Frog, Common Frog, Exploding Frog, Frog Trophy, Frog trophy, Frogs, Highlands Frog, Leap Frog, Puddle Jumper Trophy Frog, Reality Hopper, Sea Frog, Tree Frog, Trophy Frog, Wetlands Frog
Categories: Items in Sacks, Items, Pages missing IDs, Confirmations needed
[Trophy Frogs]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])-LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Bronze Trophy Frog Sack]]; [[Silver Trophy Frog Sack]]
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
Tooltip: Caught everywhere.; Bring this to Researcher Ribery on the Lotus Atoll.; * Soulbound * COMMON TROPHY
Trophy Frogs are a series of [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]] that can be caught on the [[Lotus Atoll]]. They come in four tiers, Bronze, Silver, Gold, and Diamond, and can be donated to [[Researcher Ribery]] in exchange for for [[Lotus]]. Collecting each quality of each variant of Trophy Frogs grants rewards.
[[Trophy Chance]] increases the chance to catch Trophy Frogs.
## Obtaining
Trophy Frogs can be occasionally caught while fishing on the [[Lotus Atoll]], with different requirements to catch each Trophy Frog. There are four tiers of each Trophy Frog: Bronze, Silver, Gold, and Diamond.
The [[Trophy Chance]] [[Stats|Stat]] can be used to increase the chance of Trophy Frogs being fished up. The Midas Lure perk from [[Marigold]] and Radiant Fisher perk from [[Gemma]] only apply to Gold and Diamond [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]] respectively.
When the player catches something while [[Fishing]], all Trophy Frogs that have their requirements met for are rolled as possible catches, starting from the lowest to highest catch chance.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] If no Trophy Frogs are caught, normal [[Fishing]] loot is rolled.
If the player has not caught a specific tier before, every 101st and 601st catch for each unique Trophy Frog, is guaranteed to be Gold and Diamond respectively due to [[Pity and RNG Meters|Pity]]. The Exploding Frog and Puddle Jumper are both exceptions, being guaranteed on the 51st and 301st catch instead.
### Tier Chances
When a Trophy Frog is caught, the game rolls for which Trophy Frog and tier to give the player. The rarest Trophy Frog rolls first. If Trophy Frogs have the same chance, they are rolled in alphabetical order.
Once the Trophy Frog is chosen, the game attempts to roll the Diamond tier first. If the Diamond tier is not chosen, Gold is rolled next, and so on. If the first three tiers are not granted, Bronze is guaranteed. The [[Charm]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] applies to each of these chances, except Bronze.
- Diamond: 0.2% (0.224% with [[Charm|Charm VI]])
- Gold: 2% (2.24% with [[Charm|Charm VI]])
- Silver: 25% (28% with [[Charm|Charm VI]])
- Bronze: 100%
Overall, the chance for each tier is as follows. The values below are exact.
- Diamond: 0.2% (1/500) (0.224% with [[Charm|Charm VI]](1/~446.4))
- Gold: 1.996% (1/~50) (2.2349824% with [[Charm|Charm VI]] (1/~44.74))
- Silver: 24.451% (1/~4) (27.311484928% with [[Charm|Charm VI]] (1/~3.66))
- Bronze: 73.353% (1/~1.3) (70.229532672% with [[Charm|Charm VI]] (1/~1.42))

## Usage
### Rewards
Catching individual tiers of Trophy Frogs can earn rewards from [[Researcher Ribery]].
Additionally, catching Gold and Diamond Trophy Frogs yields 15 [[Bits]] and 30 [[Bits]] respectively, increased by the player's [[Bits]] multiplier.

### SkyBlock XP
Catching Bronze, Silver, Gold and Diamond of a specific Trophy Frog for the first time grants 3, 6, 12 and 24 [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]] respectively.
Catching a higher tier also awards the [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]] of lower tiers.
### Feeding Tewtil
Bronze and Silver Trophy Frogs can be fed to [[Tewtil|Tewtils]] to make them explode and gain rewards.

# Lost & Found
Categories: Jerry, Mechanics, Storages
Lost & Found
Lost & Found is a storage for items and coins found in [[Jerry]] on the [[Private Island]].
However, sometimes when a Server Restarts (example), if you lost an item you might not be able to retrieve it. Do not rely on this.
## Usage
Lost & Found storage serves as a storage for items and coins that couldn't be directly refunded to the player. It can be opened at any time by clicking the slot below deliveries.
Known ways items or coins may end up there include:
- [[Bazaar]] buy/sell order claim
- [[Auction House]]
- Leaving the lobby before the item bought from [[Dark Auction]] is claimed
## Description
"Apparently you lost some items or coins.
Available pickups: <0>
Click to view!"
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Unknown Date|Added Lost & Found.|

# 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat
Aliases: Anniversary Balloon Hat, Anniversary Balloon Hat (5th), Aqua 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat, Balloon Hat, Balloon Hat (5th), Balloon Hat (disambiguation), Black 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat, Green 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat, Lime 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat, Orange 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat, Pink 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat, Purple 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat, Red 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat, Yellow 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat
Categories: Legacy items, Special items, Accessories, Armor
Legacy Item This item is no longer obtainable other than trading with players.
[5th Anniversary Balloon Hat]
Type: Hatccessory ([[Hatcessories]])
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Simon]]
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BALLOON_HAT_2024
Tooltip: Magic Find: +1; Show your pride for SkyBlock's 5th Anniversary with a balloon!; Ability: Party Time!; Gain +1&#xE003; Intelligence while on your head for each SkyBlock year you've been playing.; Obtained: 2024; SPECIAL HATCESSORY
The 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Hatcessories|Hatcessory]] that was given out during the 5th [[SkyBlock Anniversary]].
## Obtaining
The 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat was distributed by [[Simon]] during the 5th [[SkyBlock Anniversary]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]].
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Collecting Balloon Hat|[NPC] Simon: It's time for celebration! [NPC] Simon: SkyBlock is turning 5 years old today! [NPC] Simon: Time sure flies when you're grinding SkyBlock, eh? You claimed a [Expression] 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat!|
|Another player collects Balloon Hat|Simon gave a [Expression] 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat to <player>!|
|Attempting to claim another Balloon Hat|[NPC] Simon: I've already given you a Balloon Hat for this year! Happy Anniversary!|
## Usage
The 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat grants +1 [[Magic Find]]. It additionally provides +1 [[Intelligence]] for each [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]] played when worn. The [[Magic Find]] bonus can only be obtained once from [[Hatcessories]].
When stored in the player's [[Accessory Bag]], the 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat will count for [[Accessory Power]]. All [[Hatcessories]] count for the same [[Accessory Power]], meaning one of any kind will count for the same as all available ones combined.
## Gallery
- Red 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat.png
- Orange 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat.png
- Yellow 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat.png
- Lime 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat.png
- Green 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat.png
- Aqua 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat.png
- Purple 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat.png
- Pink 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat.png
- Black 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat.png
## Trivia
- Shortly after the event started, the NPC was disabled on the main network but not on the Alpha Network. On the Alpha Network, there was a bug that allowed players to get multiple hats by switching hubs. All hats claimed by each player were always the same color, regardless of network. A few minutes after the event started, the NPC on the main server was enabled for a few minutes with the same bug, but it was disabled again after a few more minutes. The bug was fixed on the alpha network after just over 20 minutes, and the fix was pushed to the main network about 60 minutes later. Players who claimed a Balloon Hat during the bug were able to claim another when the NPC was enabled after the bug was fixed.
- If player equips more than one Balloon Hat, only one of them will give stat bonus, resulting in only +1 [[Magic Find]] bonus.
- Hatcessories are inconsistent with their names. The 2022 Crab Hats are the only Hatcessories specifying their year of origin in their title. Crab Hats and Sloth Hats are said to be "of Celebration", while the Balloon Hats are "Anniversary".
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 10, 2024|Added 5th Anniversary Balloon Hat.|

The An Extremely Difficult Item For You To Find is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] Item that was obtainable during [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]].
## Obtaining
Looking at a [[Warden Art]] in an item frame on one's [[Private Island]] while consuming a [[Fudge Mint Core]] after using the [[Jerry Helmet]] until the following message appeared, gave the player [[An Extremely Difficult Item For You To Find]] An Extremely Difficult Item For You To Find:
THINK! Think Jerry, THINK!
## Trivia
- Several hints were given ahead of time on how to obtain this item. They were as follows:
1. This item can be found using other items.
2. This item can be found using other Honk and Seek items.
3. This item can only be found on your Private Island.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 1, 2025|Added An Extremely Difficult Item For You To Find.|

The Armor of the Rising Sun is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Armor]] Set which was obtained from [[Clerk Seraphine|Seraphine]], along with the [[Staff of the Rising Sun]] when the [[Resistance Movement|Resistance]] voting ended.
## Obtaining
It was obtained from [[Clerk Seraphine|Seraphine]] (along with the [[Staff of the Rising Sun]]), after the [[Resistance Leader]] was elected, and before the [[Dante]] fight started.
There was also an 8 hour playtime requirement to collect this set.
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Helmet of the Rising Sun ([[Armor of the Rising Sun]]); [[Health]]: +10 [[Defense]]: +10|Chestplate of the Rising Sun ([[Armor of the Rising Sun]]); [[Health]]: +15 [[Defense]]: +15|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Leggings of the Rising Sun ([[Armor of the Rising Sun]]); [[Health]]: +12 [[Defense]]: +12|Boots of the Rising Sun ([[Armor of the Rising Sun]]); [[Health]]: +8 [[Defense]]: +8|
[Total]
[Armor of the Rising Sun Health: +45 Defense: +45]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Power of the Resistance; Provides a damage bonus while fighting against Dante, and reduces damage taken from Dante.]
During the resistance, and when battling against [[Dante]], this armor set gave +1 [[Health]], [[Intelligence]] and [[Strength]], per vote the player received from the Resistance Leader voting.
## Tips
- Reforging this to [[Reforging|Necrotic]] or [[Reforging|Wise]] is recommended, as the [[Staff of the Rising Sun]] ability scales with [[Intelligence]]
## Trivia
- In Technoblade's resistance stream, the armor set seemed to be bugged, giving him the Full Set Bonus for each piece rather than all of them combined. This resulted in him having a ridiculous amount of [[Health]], [[Intelligence]] and [[Strength]].
- Players were able to obtain multiple sets of the armor and [[Staff of the Rising Sun]] if they went back to Seraphine after the 0.11.117 minor update introducing the resistance_equipment content/internal advancement.
- The helmet's texture was the same as the [[Jungle Heart]].
- The image on the Helmet likely depicts a sunrise.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 12, 2021|Added the Armor of the Rising Sun.|
|Apr 30, 2021|Changed the [[Health]] and [[Defense]] stats from 20/30/25/15 to 10/15/12/8 and removed the 150 [[Intelligence]] boost from each piece.|

The Auger Rod was a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] unlockable at [[Ice|Ice X (10)]]. It required [[Fishing|Fishing XXV (25)]] to be used. It broke nearby ice where it is cast. However, the rod did not break any ice blocks if the user was sneaking, excluding the block it was directly on. It increased the [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] of [[Sea Creatures|winter sea creatures]] by 200%.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Ice X (10) ([[Ice]])|1x [[Yeti Rod]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Packed Ice]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x32 ×4 + [[Yeti Rod]] → Auger Rod|
## Usage
Fishing Rod:
Auger Rod [[Damage]]: +135 [[Strength]]: +90 [[Fishing Speed]]: +110
[Effects]
[Breaks nearby ice blocks (Ice) where it lands.; Triples the chance of the Sea Creatures that you spawn to be winter Sea Creatures.]

Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Animates the color of an armor piece between #048F95 and #088D97!; MYTHIC DYE
Aurora Dye is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Animated [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
The Aurora Dye could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on December 24, 2024. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 375 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and, unlike other Fire Sales, had an unlimited stock, with 46,396 sold.
## Usage
The Aurora Dye can be applied to a vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its color to animate between ⁠ #048F95 and ⁠ #088D97. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.
### Colors

# Bag of Cash
Aliases: Bag Of Cash
Categories: Legacy items, Rare items, Items, Dwarven Mines

Type: Item

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BAG_OF_CASH

The Bag of Cash was a very rare drop from [[Ghost|Ghosts]] that spawn in [[The Mist]], a sub location within the [[Dwarven Mines]]. This item can be sold to [[Shops]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]]. Currently, this item is not naturally obtainable, unless you buy it from the [[Auction House]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
The Bag of Cash is no longer obtainable. It used to be a very rare drop from [[Ghost|Ghosts]] that spawn in [[The Mist]].
## Usage
The Bag of Cash can be sold to [[NPC|NPCs]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Trivia
- Ghosts can no longer drop the Bag of Cash, instead they have a chance to drop 1,000,000 [[Coins]]. This is likely to be preventative of duplication, or preventing the potentially game-breaking money-making method involving loot-sharing the Bag of Cash.
## History

|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Bag of Cash.|
|May 17, 2021|Ghosts no longer drop the Bag of Cash.|

Basket of Hope From the Great Potato War is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Mementos|Memento]] from [[The Great Potato War]].
## Obtaining
The Basket of Hope From the Great Potato War was rewarded to players in the top 1000 in the [[Potato|Potato Collection]] by the end of [[The Great Potato War]]. Players who are awarded the item have their name on the item as well as their position in the top 1000. This item is now unobtainable and will probably not be added back into the game.
## Trivia
- This is currently the item with second longest name in the game, counting 40 characters (including spaces), just after "[[Travel Scrolls|Travel Scroll to Spider's Den Top of Nest]]", which has 41 characters.
- The item is designed to look like a basket containing potatoes.
