# Bedrock
Categories: Legacy items, Vanilla objects, Legendary items, Blocks, Pages with non-auctionable items with valid auctionable item IDs, Glitches
Legacy Item This item is no longer obtainable other than trading with players.
Bedrock is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Bedrock.
[Bedrock]
Type: Block
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: I guess you have this now.
Source: Bug
[Block Details]
Location: [[Private Island]]
Tool ([[Tools]]): None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BEDROCK
Tooltip: I guess you have this now.; * Co-op Soulbound * LEGENDARY
Bedrock is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] unobtainable, unbreakable block. It can be found on the spawn point of the player's [[Private Island]], on [[The Garden]] as a bottom border for every plot, and it will appear after mining materials on certain public islands, indicating that block cannot be mined further until it regenerates. It also appears in [[The End]] and in the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]] as a decoration, as well as within [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] and the [[Crystal Hollows]] acting as a border.
## Trivia
- The former admin 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Minikloon once put a Bedrock block on the [[Auction House]] with a starting price of 2B [[Coins]].
  - The auction went for 2B [[Coins]] because Minikloon knew that no one had that much money at that time.
  - This is the only time that Bedrock was sold on the [[Auction House]] until January 15, 2021.
  - This version of the item had no lore, excluding the rarity.
- For a short time before the Dwarven Mines update, it was possible to break Bedrock on [[Private Island|Private Islands]] because of the mechanics of the new mining system.
  - The lore description was changed from "Please contact Jayavarmen#8817 and explain how on earth you got this item, tyvm" to "I guess you have this now." after this was patched.
## Gallery
- Bedrock being auctioned by Minikloon.
- On May 13, 2020 somebody placed a bedrock block near bank at Hub 6.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Bedrock.|
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Bedrock was inadvertently made breakable on private islands with a high enough [[Breaking Power]].|
|Jan 15, 2021|Bedrock can no longer be broken on private islands.|
|Unknown Date|Removed extra line in lore.|
|Jun 9, 2026|Bedrock is now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Jun 9, 2026|The base item of Bedrock is now [[Paper]], making it no longer placeable. However, the item model remains [[Bedrock]] Bedrock.|
|Jun 9, 2026|Bedrock can no longer be sold to the NPCs ([[NPC]]).|

# Crab Hat of Celebration
Aliases: Aqua Crab Hat of Celebration, Aqua Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Edition, Black Crab Hat of Celebration, Black Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Edition, Crab Hat, Crab Hat Of Celebration, Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Edition, Crab Hat of Celebration Aqua, Crab Hat of Celebration Black, Crab Hat of Celebration Green, Crab Hat of Celebration Lime, Crab Hat of Celebration Orange, Crab Hat of Celebration Pink, Crab Hat of Celebration Purple, Crab Hat of Celebration Red, Crab Hat of Celebration Yellow, Crab Hats Of Celebration, Crab Hats of Celebration, Green Crab Hat of Celebration, Green Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Edition, Lime Crab Hat of Celebration, Lime Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Edition, Orange Crab Hat of Celebration, Orange Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Edition, Pink Crab Hat of Celebration, Pink Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Edition, Purple Crab Hat of Celebration, Purple Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Edition, Red Crab Hat of Celebration, Red Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Edition, Yellow Crab Hat of Celebration, Yellow Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Edition

Tooltip: Magic Find: +1; Ability: Party Time!; Gain +1&#xE003; Intelligence while on your head for each SkyBlock year you've been playing.; Obtained: 2020; SPECIAL HATCESSORY
The Crab Hat of Celebration is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Hatcessories|Hatccessory]] that was given out during a [[SkyBlock Anniversary]].
## Obtaining
The Crab Hat of Celebration was distributed by [[Simon]] during the [[SkyBlock Anniversary|Party Time!]] event in 2020, which marked the first real-life anniversary of [[SkyBlock|Hypixel SkyBlock]].
In 2022, [[Simon]] appeared during the [[SkyBlock Anniversary]] near the [[Colosseum]] to distribute Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Editions, which were animated unlike their 2020 counterpart.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Collecting Crab Hat 2020|Simon gave a [color] Crab Hat to <player>!|
|Collecting Crab Hat 2022|Simon gave a [Color] Crab Hat of Celebration - 2022 Edition to <player>!|
|Attempting to claim another Crab Hat 2020|You have already collected a hat for this year!|
|Attempting to claim another Crab Hat 2022|[NPC] Simon: I've already given you a Crab Hat for this year! Happy Anniversary!|
## Usage
The Crab Hat of Celebration grants +1 [[Magic Find]]. It additionally provides +1 [[Intelligence]] for each [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]] played when worn. The [[Magic Find]] bonus can only be obtained once from [[Hatcessories]].
When stored in the player's [[Accessory Bag]], the Crab Hat of Celebration will count for [[Accessory Power]]. All [[Hatcessories]] count for the same [[Accessory Power]], meaning one of any kind will count for the same as all available ones combined.
Additionally, the Crab Hat of Celebration gives the following cosmetic effect when worn:
- Rainbow potion particles appear around the player.
- Fireworks occasionally launch from the player's head.
- For the 2022 Edition, the texture is animated. See § Gallery below.
## Trivia
- The color of the hat was randomly selected when it was claimed, like the [[Century Cakes|Century Cake]] or the expression of [[Sloth Hat of Celebration]].
- The party hats worn by the crabs were white in 2020 and black in 2022.
- Players can only acquire a Crab Hat on one [[Profile]].
- Black Crab Hats are generally worth more on the [[Auction House]] because they are more visually pleasing, and the name blends in with the background in the tooltip.
- The Crab Hats, [[Sloth Hat of Celebration|Sloth Hats]], and the [[Pulse Ring]] are the only [[Accessories]] that give [[Magic Find]] without [[Enrichments]].
- As a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] accessory, the Crab Hat can be [[Enrichments|enriched]].
- When the 2022 edition was released, the stats, enrichments, and [[Accessory Power]] of different editions stacked with each other.
  - The stacking has been removed since a certain point before Aug 19, 2022.
- The amount of [[Intelligence]] gained depends only on the number of [[Time Systems|SkyBlock years]] that have passed since the player created their oldest profile, regardless of the player's activity during this time. The current maximum, which applies to players who joined during the first SkyBlock year, is 521? (Early Autumn 19th, 522, 02:01 AM | Click to Purge the Cache).
- The Crab Hat was the first [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] accessory.
- Hatccessories are inconsistent with their names. The 2022 Crab Hats are the only Hatcessories specifying their year of origin in their title. Crab Hats and Sloth Hats are said to be "of Celebration", while the Balloon Hats are "Anniversary".

# Dante's Talisman
Aliases: Dante Talisman
Categories: Legacy items, Common items, Accessories, The Uprising, Talisman

Tooltip: Accessory Power: +3; Official medal proving your eternal support for Dante.; Can be shown to goons in place of a Happy Mask for entry into official buildings.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; COMMON ACCESSORY
Dante's Talisman is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that granted access to [[Official Buildings]] while [[Dante]] was mayor.
## Obtaining
### Dante's Reign
During Dante's reign, Dante's Talisman was purchasable from [[Dante]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
### Stranded
On [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]], Dante's Talisman can be purchased from [[Stranded|Villagers]] for 35 [[Enchanted Emerald]] at Villager level 3.
## Usage
Similar to the [[Happy Mask]], Dante's Talisman granted access to the [[Official Buildings]]. It worked while held in the player's inventory or stored in their [[Accessory Bag]].
If one player in a co-op had a Dante's Talisman, all players in the co-op shared the ability to enter the [[Official Buildings]]. This also applied between profiles.
## Trivia
- While Dante's Talisman is no longer obtainable, [[Dante's Ring]] was introduced to provide the same [[Accessory Power]] for players that joined after [[Dante]] was defeated. Only one of the two counts towards [[Accessory Power]] while in the [[Accessory Bag]].

The Editor's Pencil is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item.
## Obtaining
The Editor's Pencil is an item rewarded to editors of the Official Hypixel Wiki who went 'above and beyond'.
## Usage
The Editor's Pencil used to send a link in chat to a random random page on the Official Hypixel Wiki. This functionality was later removed.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Right-clicking the Pencil (before the Official Wiki's closure)|Click HERE to see a random Wiki page!|
## Trivia
- 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] Messisteve invented the lore of the Editor's Pencil.
- The Editor's Pencil was originally going to be called the Ancient Pencil, but was renamed in late 2021.
## See also
- [[Wiki Journal]]

Tooltip: Health: +40; Defense: +35; Intelligence: +5; Full Set Bonus: Nether Lord (0\ 4); Grants immunity to all damage from Lava and Fire.; Walking over Lava creates Obsidian under your feet.; Disabled in some zones and near Fishing Hotspots.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 8.; EPIC HELMET
Ember Armor is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Armor]] set that allows the player to turn nearby lava into [[Obsidian]].
## Obtaining
### Magma Cube Boss
The [[Magma Boss]] had a small chance of dropping a random piece of this armor upon death.
### Crafting
The armor can be crafted using [[Ember Fragment|Ember Fragments]], which had a chance to drop from the [[Magma Boss]]. While [[Ember Fragment|Ember Fragments]] are no longer obtainable except via trading with players, the armor pieces can still be crafted.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ember Helmet ([[Ember Armor]])|None|5x [[Ember Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Ember Fragment]] ×5 → [[Ember Armor]]|
|Ember Chestplate ([[Ember Armor]])|None|8x [[Ember Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Ember Fragment]] ×8 → [[Ember Armor]]|
|Ember Leggings ([[Ember Armor]])|None|7x [[Ember Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Ember Fragment]] ×7 → [[Ember Armor]]|
|Ember Boots ([[Ember Armor]])|None|4x [[Ember Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Ember Fragment]] ×4 → [[Ember Armor]]|
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Ember Helmet ([[Ember Armor]]); [[Health]]: +40 [[Defense]]: +35 [[Intelligence]]: +5|Ember Chestplate ([[Ember Armor]]); [[Health]]: +65 [[Defense]]: +60 [[Intelligence]]: +10|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Ember Leggings ([[Ember Armor]]); [[Health]]: +60 [[Defense]]: +55 [[Intelligence]]: +5|Ember Boots ([[Ember Armor]]); [[Health]]: +35 [[Defense]]: +30 [[Intelligence]]: +5|
[Total]
[Ember Armor Health: +200 Defense: +180 Intelligence: +25]
[Effects]
[Full Set Bonus: Nether Lord; Walking over Lava (Vanilla Objects/Blocks) creates Obsidian under your feet. You are also immune to all damage from Lava and Fire.]
### Lava Immunity
Wearing a full set of Ember Armor grants the player immunity to damage from fire and lava. It also turns nearby lava into [[Obsidian]] (except in [[Kuudra's Hollow]]).
### Kuudra Follower Chestplate
To obtain the [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Chestplate]], six players are needed. These players must be wearing [[Ember Armor]] Ember Armor or [[Rekindled Ember Armor]] with a [Lv81] or higher [[Kuudra Pet]] summoned.
- 2 players, one from each faction, must stand in the [[Stronghold]].
- 2 players in the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]] must stand in the [[Dragontail Townsquare]].
- 2 players in the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] must stand in the [[Scarleton Plaza]].
The two players standing in the [[Stronghold]], as well as the other players on the server, can then speak to [[Arch]] to claim the chestplate.
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|Notes & Versions|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Smoldering Ember Skin]]|[[Smoldering Ember Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Summer 2024 - 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|This skin is animated!|
|[[Smoldering Ember Skin]]|[[Smoldering Ember Skin]]|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|(same as above)||
## Tips
- As this set is now unobtainable, two or more pieces of [[Magma Lord Armor]] can also be used instead to grant lava immunity in the [[Crimson Isle]] and [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Fixed being able to mine [[Obsidian]] from the Nether Lord ability.|

Ember Ash Armor is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Armor]] set obtained from older versions of the [[Flamebreaker Armor]]. The set gives +60 [[Mining Speed]] and +20 [[Mining Fortune]]. It requires [[Hard Stone|Hard Stone V (5)]] to use.
## Obtaining
After the Crystal Hollows (0.12) update, the original [[Flamebreaker Armor]] sets that were "released" for a day were changed to Ember Ash Armor, and as such cannot be obtained other than through [[Trading]] with other players or on the [[Auction House]].
### Crafting
Note: This is the crafting recipe for the original [[Flamebreaker Armor]], which was later changed to Ember Ash Armor, but the recipe had been removed.
Due to the fact that the recipes for the helmet and leggings are the same, these items were only obtainable through quick crafting. Therefore, only ranked users could craft these two pieces.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Flamebreaker Helmet ([[Flamebreaker Armor]])|None|7x [[Coal]]|Recipe: [[Coal]] ×7 → [[Flamebreaker Armor]]|
|Flamebreaker Chestplate ([[Flamebreaker Armor]])|None|8x [[Coal]]|Recipe: [[Coal]] ×8 → [[Flamebreaker Armor]]|
|Flamebreaker Leggings ([[Flamebreaker Armor]])|None|7x [[Coal]]|Recipe: [[Coal]] ×7 → [[Flamebreaker Armor]]|
|Flamebreaker Boots ([[Flamebreaker Armor]])|None|7x [[Coal]]|Recipe: [[Coal]] ×7 → [[Flamebreaker Armor]]|

|Leggings|Boots|
|Ember Ash Leggings ([[Ember Ash Armor]]); [[Mining Speed]]: +15 [[Mining Fortune]]: +5|Ember Ash Boots ([[Ember Ash Armor]]); [[Mining Speed]]: +15 [[Mining Fortune]]: +5|

## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
Ember Fragments used to be obtainable by killing the [[Magma Cube Boss]]. If the player did not drop a piece of [[Ember Armor]] or an [[Ember Rod]], they would receive 1-3 Ember Fragments.

The Ember Rod is an unobtainable [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Swords|Sword]] which used to be dropped by the [[Magma Cube Boss]] in the [[Blazing Fortress]]. It requires [[Combat|Combat VIII (8)]] to use and can now only be obtained through the [[Auction House]] or [[Trading]].
## Obtaining
The Ember Rod is unobtainable. Prior to the 0.7.8 update, the Ember Rod would drop to a random player: you must have dealt at least 1 damage to roll for an Ember Rod. Only one player could obtain an Ember Rod per boss.
## Usage
Sword:
Ember Rod [[Damage]]: +80 [[Strength]]: +35 [[Intelligence]]: +50
[Effects]
[Ability: Fire Blast RIGHT CLICK; Shoots 3 fireballs which deal 30 damage in rapid succession in front of you!; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 150; Cooldown: 30s; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 1]
## Trivia
- The Ember Rod is directly related to another Hypixel Minigame, Mega Walls. The ability of the Blaze Kit in Mega Walls is the same as the Ember Rod.
- Upon SkyBlock's initial release, the Ember Rod was considered to be one of the best swords in the game, falling behind the [[Leaping Sword]] and [[Pigman Sword]] and costing over 200,000 [[Coins]] on the Auction House.
  - 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] TimeDeo and his [[Co-op]] members abused the high cost of the Ember Rod and the fact that the Magma Cube Boss's spawn times used to vary between servers to become the first Co-op with 50,000,000 [[Coins]].
- The Ember Rod could previously be crafted into [[Blaze Powder]] using the vanilla crafting grid in the [[Player|Inventory]]. A later update forbade non-vanilla items from being used in vanilla crafting recipes.
- The Fire Blast ability doesn't work on [[Enderman|Endermen]], as projectiles cannot hit them.

Item ID: ENCHANTED_BOOKSHELF
Tooltip: Legacy Item; This item is no longer used in the game. It can be exchanged in your Legacy Trades menu - accessed with \ legacytrades.; * Co-op Soulbound * UNCOMMON
An Enchanted Bookshelf was an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] block that could be placed on [[Private Island]]. It can now be traded for 1 [[Magic For Pets In A Hurry]] in the [[Legacy Trades]] menu.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
The Enchanted Bookshelf used to be craftable with the following recipe:
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Sugar Cane VI (6) ([[Sugar Cane]])|6x [[Enchanted Oak Log]] 6x [[Enchanted Paper]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Oak Log]] ×6 + [[Enchanted Paper]] x2 ×3 → Enchanted Bookshelf|
## Usage
An Enchanted Bookshelf can be traded for 1 [[Magic For Pets In A Hurry]] in the [[Legacy Trades]] menu, accessed from /legacytrades.
### Removed Usage
It used to be placeable on the player's [[Private Island]] by an [[Enchantment Table]] to grant 15 Bookshelf Power, allowing access to better [[Enchantments]]. A Bookshelf Power of 20 gave access to all [[Enchantments]]. See Enchanting mechanics § Bookshelf placement on the Minecraft Wiki for information about the placement that was required at the time.
## Trivia
- An Enchanted Bookshelf could only be crafted with [[Enchanted Oak Log]]. Other types of wood do not work, even though a regular [[Bookshelf]] can be crafted with any combination of planks.
- Only [[Enchanted Bookshelf]] 2 Enchanted Bookshelves were needed to access all [[Enchantments]].
- Prior to version 0.10, placing additional Enchanted Bookshelves would raise the amount of [[Experience]] levels required to apply the strongest [[Enchantments]]. However, the Enchantments would still only appear at level 64.
- Enchanted Bookshelves cannot be [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]]. If one is already recombobulated, it will be forcibly reverted to its base rarity.

Enchanted Hay Bale is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] crafting material, that used to be unlocked at [[Wheat|Wheat VII (7)]].
## Obtaining
Enchanted Hay Bale (Old) can no longer be obtained. Prior to 0.20.8, it was obtainable through following methods:
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wheat VII (7) ([[Wheat]])|144x (2 stacks plus 16 (144 in total)) [[Hay Bale]]|Recipe: [[Hay Bale]] x16 ×9 → [[Enchanted Hay Bale]]|
### Pests
Enchanted Hay Bale was a guaranteed drop from killing [[Fly]] Pests on [[The Garden]]. [[Farming Fortune]] and [[Crop Fortune|Wheat Fortune]] acted as a multiplier for the amount dropped, and the [[Reforging|Beady]] reforge increased the amount of drops by 3.
## Usage
### Legacy Trades
[[Enchanted Hay Bale (Old)]] 1 Enchanted Hay Bale (Old) can be traded for 8 [[Enchanted Wheat]] in the /legacytrades menu.

# French Bread
Categories: Legacy items, Special items, Items, Hypixel Gifts
Legacy Item This item is no longer obtainable other than trading with players.
[French Bread]
Type: Item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FRENCH_BREAD
Tooltip: It is known that scientists and other experts always find ways to disagree on every subject known to humankind. However, they can all agree on one thing - that this is the greatest meal ever created in history.; Awarded to those who participated in Dungeons on the Alpha Hypixel Network.; SPECIAL MEMENTO
The French Bread is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Mementos|Memento]]. It was added to the game after many users spammed each message with French Bread reactions in the Hypixel Discord Server after the #admin-lounge channel, which was renamed to #⭐mvp-lounge⭐, was opened to 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] and above.
It currently has no function in the game other than for decoration purposes.
## Obtaining
The French Bread was only obtainable by defeating [[Bonzo]] or [[Scarf]] on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] during testing, and it was delivered to the player's personal island via [[Jerry]].
Players are unable to obtain this item directly, except for YouTubers, Hypixel Player Council, Staff and 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] who had access to the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]].
## Trivia
- Players who defeated both [[Bonzo]] and [[Scarf]] on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] received 2 French Breads.

Frog Dye is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Animated [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
The Frog Dye could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on July 19, 2025. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 375 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 60,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Frog Dye can be applied to a vanilla [[Armor]] piece by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its color to animate between ⁠ #64D400 and ⁠ #65D400. It costs 100 [[Bits]] to combine the two items.

Item ID: FROSTY_SNOW_BALL
Tooltip: COMMON
The Frosty Snowball is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] discontinued, unobtainable item. They served as a fuel for [[Frosty the Snow Cannon]].
## Obtaining
Frosty Snowballs cannot be obtained by any means. They were previously available in the UI of [[Frosty the Snow Cannon]], which was removed two days later. Currently, the only way to obtain Frosty Snowballs is to buy them from the [[Auction House]]. The steps to obtaining this item is:
1. Opening the UI of the snow cannon
2. Taking the snowball out
The snow cannon used to have an inventory for the ammo.
## History

|Dec 19, 2019|Removed Frosty Snowball.|
|Jun 19, 2026|Frosty Snowball is now Co-op Soulbound.|

The Fudge Mint Core is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] Item that was obtainable during [[A Honk and Seek Adventure]].
## Obtaining
The Fudge Mint Core had a 10% (1 in 10 kills) chance to drop from killing any tier of [[Voidgloom Seraph]].
## Usage
Looking at a [[Warden Art]] in an item frame on one's [[Private Island]] while consuming a [[Fudge Mint Core]] Fudge Mint Core after using the [[Jerry Helmet]] until the following message appeared, gave the player [[An Extremely Difficult Item For You To Find]]:
THINK! Think Jerry, THINK!
## Trivia
- The Fudge Mint Core is a reference to the [[Judgement Core]].

# God Potion (Legacy)
Aliases: Legacy God Potion
Categories: Legacy items, Special items, Items, Confirmations needed
Legacy Item This item is no longer obtainable other than trading with players.
[God Potion]
Type: Item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOD_POTION
Tooltip: Legacy; Consuming this potion gives the player 24 hours of all positive potion effects!; Right-click to consume!; SPECIAL
The Legacy God Potions are a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] consumable item with the functionality of regular [[God Potion]] bought before the [[City Projects/Bartender's Brewery|City Project/Bartender's Brewery]]. They work like a regular God Potion, but last for 24 hours regardless of Alchemy level.
## Usage
The Legacy God Potions can be consumed via right-click, which will grant the player most positive potion effects, including [[Jerry Candy]]'s effect, for 24 hours. This duration is paused on players' [[Private Island]], but the effect will still be active. The effect is not consumed on death[Confirm (Confirmation needed; is this true for the legacy form of God Potion?; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]. Players can apply [[Mixins]] in a [[Brewing Stand]] for additional effects[Confirm].
## See Also
- [[God Potion]]
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added God Potion.|
|Sep 9, 2020|God Potion now includes brews|
|Sep 10, 2020|God Potions can no longer be placed on chests.|
|Jan 14, 2021|Added [[Jerry Candy]]'s effects to God Potion.|
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Spelunker IV ([[Spelunker Potion]]) to God Potion.|
|Mar 12, 2021|Newly bought God Potions now last only 12 hours, but gain up to 12 bonus hours based on [[Alchemy]] level.|
|Mar 12, 2021|Existing God Potions are now turned into legacy God Potions, which last 24 hours as before.|
|Mar 12, 2021|Reduced buy price by 500 [[Bits]] (from 2000 to 1500).|
|Mar 12, 2021|Effects of God Potion are now extended by the [[Parrot Pet]].|
|Mar 12, 2021|God Potions can now be improved with [[Mixins]] in a [[Brewing Stand]].|
